# Act XV of 1995: Import Duties (Amendment) (No. 2) Act, 1995
UGO MIF'SUO BONNICI (L.S.)
6th June. 1995
ACT No. XV or I ftS
(I) This Act may be cited as thc Imporl Duties
(2) The following pro visions of this Act shall bc deemed to 3. For section 4 of the principal Act there shall be substituted the
following:
...
4. (1) There shall be levied and collected by the " Dutid to be Ie"",d Complroller of Customs on account of the Government the
import duties shown in the 'Imporl Duty' column of the ""!caod
Customs Tariff upon all goods chargeable with such duties ~roIle'
and enumerated in the fint column of the said Tariff, which
ofCUSlomS.
are grown, produced or manufactured in countries, other
than those in the Community and which shall be imported
into Maha or taken oul of bond for use or consumption in
Malta.
(2) The import duties shown in the 'Imporl DUlY'
column shall not apply 10 goods which are grown , produced
or manufactured in the Community and which shall be
imported into Malta or taken out of bond for use or
consumption in Malta provided that such goods satisfy the
rules of origin as in force from time to time in accordance with
any agreement made between the Government of Malta and
thc Community.
(3) Notwithstanding the provisions of subsections
(J) and (2) of this section, the Comptroller of Customs may,
with the concurrence of the importer, apply a flat rate of ten
per cent import duty on goods of a non-commercial nature
received by individual persons in consignments where the
value of. such goods does not exceed fifty Maltcsc liri, and on
goods of a non--oommercial nature imported by travellen in
their luggage whcre the value of such goods does not exceed
hundred Maltese Liri, in either case being goods other than
spirits, tobacco or tobacco products.
(4) In the computation and collection of import
duty, the Comptroller may round up or down the value ofthe
goods and the amount of duty chargeable thereon 10 the
ncarest Maltese lira.'·.
4. Section 8 of thc principal Act shall be amended as follows:
(a) for subsection (I) thereof there shall be substituted the
following:
"( I) The cost of goods, for the purpose of ascertaining
thc amount of thc ad ~alorem duty payable thereon , shall be
calculated on the price chargcd for the said goods by the
vcndor thereof as vcrified by the genuinc invoice of the goods,
with the addilion of the cost of transport and insurance
necessary for the importation of the goods into Malta and
where the consignment consists of goods subject to different
rates of duty this additional cost attributable to transport shall
be apportioned in direct proportion 10 the invoiced value of
the respective goods:
Provided that where the O>mptrollerofCustoms has
reason to believe that the invoice does not provide a proper
basis for the assessment of the correct amount of duty on the
goods, the duty may be calculated on a value which shalt be
established in accordance with the provisions of the Third
Schedule to this Act:
Provided further that in the case of goods to which
section 6 applies the ad valorem duty shalt be payable -
(i) where the goods are sold. on the price paid by
the purchaser; or
(ii) where the goods are disposed of otherwise than
by the sale, on the valueofthegoods as detennined by the
Comptroller of Customs at the time of disposal:
Provided further that where in the Notes to the Tariff
it is provided that a minimum value of an article is to be taken
for the purpose of calculating duty , the value established by
tbis subsection may not be below the value as stated in such
Notes,";
Immediately after subsection (4) of section 15 of the principal

commercial road vehicles. that such packages had not

Provided that in such cases if immediately before the
examination of the imported goods by the Comptroller the
foreign and local seals are found to be intact by the
Comptroller, this fact may be considered as satisfaC10ry
evidence for the purpose of this subsection; or", 7: For the Firsl &:hedu1e to the principal Act there shall be

8. In subparagraph (iii) of paragraph (q) in item 11 of the
Second Schedule to the principal Act, for the words "LmI3" there shall
be substituted the words '"LmS".
9, The enactments in the First Column in Schedule B to this Act
shall effect subject to the amendments appearing relative thereto in the
Second Column of the said Schedule.
10. Pursuant 10 section 12A of the principal Act, the new duty or
increased rale of dUlY leviable in accordance with this Act, shall be
leviable on any goods in relatiolllo which a perfect bill of entry has been
made but have nol been released up to the time of the issue by the
President of the relative Proclamation under the Revenue Safeguard
Act, relative to this Act.
11. Where under any law a penalty. whether civil or criminal. is
due by any person as a consequence of an offence or breach of any such
law by such person before the coming into force of this Act, and the
penalty is calculated as a proportion of, or a multiple of, the im port duty
due or leviable on any goods, such penalty shall be calculated on the
import dUlies on such goods in accordance with the principal Act as in
force before the coming into force of this Act.
